<a id="threading"></a>
## Threading: CoreDispatcher → DispatcherQueueUWP (replace this):```csharp
await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => StatusText.Text = "Done");
```WinUI 3:```csharp
DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() => StatusText.Text = "Done");
DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(DispatcherQueuePriority.High, () => ProgressBar.Value = 100);
```Cache the queue off the UI thread via `DispatcherQueue.GetForCurrentThread()`. UWP's ASTA reentrancy protection is gone — watch for reentrancy in async code that pumps messages. See the official [threading guide](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/apps/windows-app-sdk/migrate-to-windows-app-sdk/guides/threading).

425.         /// <summary>
426.         /// Enforces a fictitious content publisher's rules for bandwidth maximums based on HDCP protection levels.
427.         /// </summary>
428.         /// <param name="protection">Protection level to use when imposing bandwidth restriction.</param>
429.         /// <param name="ams">AdaptiveMediaSource on which to impose restrictions</param>
430.         private async void SetMaxBitrateForProtectionLevel(HdcpProtection? protection, AdaptiveMediaSource ams)
431.         {
432.             // TODO[migrate-008]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
433.             await this.Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
434.             {
435.                 EffectiveHdcpProtectionText.Text = protection.ToString();
436. 
437.                 if (ams != null && ams.AvailableBitrates.Count > 1)
438.                 {
439.                     // Get a sorted list of available bitrates.
440.                     var bitrates = new List<uint>(ams.AvailableBitrates);
441.                     bitrates.Sort();
442. 
443.                     // Apply maximum bitrate policy based on a fictitious content publisher's rules.
444.                     switch (protection)
445.                     {
446.                         case HdcpProtection.OnWithTypeEnforcement:
447.                             // Allow full bitrate.
448.                             ams.DesiredMaxBitrate = bitrates[bitrates.Count - 1];
449.                             DesiredMaxBitrateText.Text = "full bitrate allowed";
450.                             break;
451.                         case HdcpProtection.On:
452.                             // When there is no HDCP Type 1, make the highest bitrate unavailable.
453.                             ams.DesiredMaxBitrate = bitrates[bitrates.Count - 2];
454.                             DesiredMaxBitrateText.Text = "highest bitrate is unavailable";
455.                             break;
456.                         case HdcpProtection.Off:
457.                         case null:
458.                         default:
459.                             // When there is no HDCP at all (Off), or the system is still trying to determine what
460.                             // HDCP protection level to apply (null), then make only the lowest bitrate available.
461.                             ams.DesiredMaxBitrate = bitrates[0];
462.                             DesiredMaxBitrateText.Text = "lowest bitrate only";
463.                             break;
464.                     }
465.                     Log($"Imposed DesiredMaxBitrate={ams.DesiredMaxBitrate} for HdcpProtection.{protection}");
466.                 }
467.             });
468.         }

12. using SDKTemplate.Models;
13. using System;
14. using System.Collections.Generic;
15. using System.Linq;
16. using Windows.ApplicationModel.DataTransfer;
17. using Windows.UI.Core;
18. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;
19. 
20. namespace SDKTemplate
21. {
22.     public partial class MainPage : Page
23.     {
24.         public const string FEATURE_NAME = "AdaptiveStreaming";
25. 
26.         List<Scenario> scenarios = new List<Scenario>
27.         {
28.             new Scenario() { Title="Simplest Adaptive Streaming", ClassType=typeof(Scenario1_SimplestAdaptiveStreaming)},
29.             new Scenario() { Title="Event Handlers", ClassType=typeof(Scenario2_EventHandlers)},
30.             new Scenario() { Title="Network Request Modification", ClassType=typeof(Scenario3_RequestModification)},
31.             new Scenario() { Title="Adaptive Streaming Tuning", ClassType=typeof(Scenario4_Tuning)},
32.             new Scenario() { Title="Metadata", ClassType=typeof(Scenario5_Metadata)},
33.             new Scenario() { Title="Ad Insertion", ClassType=typeof(Scenario6_AdInsertion)},
34.             new Scenario() { Title="Live Seekable Range", ClassType=typeof(Scenario7_LiveSeekableRange)},
35.         };
36. 
37.         public static IReadOnlyList<AdaptiveContentModel> ContentManagementSystemStub = AdaptiveContentModel.GetKnownAzureMediaServicesModels();
38.         public static AdaptiveContentModel FindContentById(int id)
39.         {
40.             return ContentManagementSystemStub.First(model => model.Id == id);
41.         }
42.     }
43. 
44.     public class Scenario
45.     {
46.         public string Title { get; set; }
47.         public Type ClassType { get; set; }
48.     }
49. 
50.     public class BitrateHelper
51.     {
52.         // Each bitrate is assigned a symbol as follows:
53.         // The bottom quartile gets 1 bar, the second quartile gets 2 bars,
54.         // the third quartile gets 3 bars, and the top quartile gets 4 bars.
55.         private Dictionary<uint, Symbol> symbolAssignment = new Dictionary<uint, Symbol>();
56. 
57.         public BitrateHelper(IEnumerable<uint> availableBitRates)
58.         {
59.             uint[] sortedBitRates = availableBitRates.ToArray();
60.             Array.Sort(sortedBitRates);
61.             int i = 0;
62.             while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 1 / 4)
63.             {
64.                 symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.OneBar);
65.             }
66.             while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 2 / 4)
67.             {
68.                 symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.TwoBars);
69.             }
70.             while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 3 / 4)
71.             {
72.                 symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.ThreeBars);
73.             }
74.             while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 4 / 4)
75.             {
76.                 symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.FourBars);
77.             }
78.         }
79. 
80.         public Symbol GetBitrateSymbol(uint currentBitrate)
81.         {
82.             Symbol symbol = Symbol.ZeroBars;
83.             if (symbolAssignment.TryGetValue(currentBitrate, out symbol))
84.             {
85.                 return symbol;
86.             }
87.             return Symbol.ZeroBars;
88.         }
89.     }
90. }
91.

22. // The Blank Page item template is documented at http://go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkId=402352&clcid=0x409
23. 
24. namespace SDKTemplate
25. {
26.     /// <summary>
27.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
28.     /// </summary>
29.     public sealed partial class MainPage : Page
30.     {
31.         public static MainPage Current;
32. 
33.         public MainPage()
34.         {
35.             this.InitializeComponent();
36. 
37.             // This is a static public property that allows downstream pages to get a handle to the MainPage instance
38.             // in order to call methods that are in this class.
39.             Current = this;
40.             SampleTitle.Text = FEATURE_NAME;
41.         }
42. 
43.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
44.         {
45.             // Populate the scenario list from the SampleConfiguration.cs file
46.             var itemCollection = new List<Scenario>();
47.             int i = 1;
48.             foreach (Scenario s in scenarios)
49.             {
50.                 itemCollection.Add(new Scenario { Title = $"{i++}) {s.Title}", ClassType = s.ClassType });
51.             }
52.             ScenarioControl.ItemsSource = itemCollection;
53. 
54.             if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
55.             {
56.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = -1;
57.             }
58.             else
59.             {
60.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = 0;
61.             }
62.         }
63. 
64.         /// <summary>
65.         /// Called whenever the user changes selection in the scenarios list.  This method will navigate to the respective
66.         /// sample scenario page.
67.         /// </summary>
68.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
69.         /// <param name="e"></param>
70.         private void ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
71.         {
72.             // Clear the status block when navigating scenarios.
73.             NotifyUser(String.Empty, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
74. 
75.             ListBox scenarioListBox = sender as ListBox;
76.             Scenario s = scenarioListBox.SelectedItem as Scenario;
77.             if (s != null)
78.             {
79.                 ScenarioFrame.Navigate(s.ClassType);
80.                 if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
81.                 {
82.                     Splitter.IsPaneOpen = false;
83.                 }
84.             }
85.         }
86. 
87.         public List<Scenario> Scenarios
88.         {
89.             get { return this.scenarios; }
90.         }
91. 
92.         /// <summary>
93.         /// Display a message to the user.
94.         /// This method may be called from any thread.
95.         /// </summary>
96.         /// <param name="strMessage"></param>
97.         /// <param name="type"></param>
98.         public void NotifyUser(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
99.         {
100.             // If called from the UI thread, then update immediately.
101.             // Otherwise, schedule a task on the UI thread to perform the update.
102.             if (Dispatcher.HasThreadAccess)
103.             {
104.                 UpdateStatus(strMessage, type);
105.             }
106.             else
107.             {
108.                 var task = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => UpdateStatus(strMessage, type));
109.             }
110.         }
111. 
112.         private void UpdateStatus(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
113.         {
114.             switch (type)
115.             {
116.                 case NotifyType.StatusMessage:
117.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Green);
118.                     break;
119.                 case NotifyType.ErrorMessage:
120.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Red);
121.                     break;
122.             }
123. 
124.             StatusBlock.Text = strMessage;
125. 
126.             // Collapse the StatusBlock if it has no text to conserve real estate.
127.             StatusBorder.Visibility = (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty) ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
128.             if (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty)
129.             {
130.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
131.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
132.             }
133.             else
134.             {
135.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
136.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
137.             }
138. 
139. 			// Raise an event if necessary to enable a screen reader to announce the status update.
140. 			var peer = FrameworkElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(StatusBlock);
141. 			if (peer != null)
142. 			{
143. 				peer.RaiseAutomationEvent(AutomationEvents.LiveRegionChanged);
144. 			}
145. 		}
146. 
147.         async void Footer_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
148.         {
149.             await Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(new Uri(((HyperlinkButton)sender).Tag.ToString()));
150.         }
151. 
152.         private void Button_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
153.         {
154.             Splitter.IsPaneOpen = !Splitter.IsPaneOpen;
155.         }
156.     }
157.     public enum NotifyType
158.     {
159.         StatusMessage,
160.         ErrorMessage
161.     };
162. }

42.         internal void Initialize(MediaPlayer mediaPlayer, 
43.             IEnumerable<AdaptiveContentModel> adaptiveContentModels,
44.             HttpClient optionalHttpClient,
45.             LogView loggerControl, 
46.             Func<Uri, HttpClient, Task<MediaPlaybackItem>> loadSourceFromUriAsync)
47.         {

96.         /// Logging functions tangential to main scenarios should specify a log view level less than Always.
97.         /// </summary>
98.         /// <param name="message">message to be logged</param>
99.         /// <param name="level">message category</param>
100.         public async void Log(string message, LogViewLoggingLevel level = LogViewLoggingLevel.Always)
101.         {
102.             if (Dispatcher.HasThreadAccess)
103.             {

105.             }
106.             else
107.             {
108.                 await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
109.                 {
110.                     LogFromUIThread(message, level);
111.                 });
112.             }
113.         }

115.         private void LogFromUIThread(string message, LogViewLoggingLevel level)
116.         {
117.             string logEntry = $"{DateTime.Now:HH:mm:ss.fff} - {message}";
118. 
119.             if (LoggingLevel <= level)
120.             {
121.                 SolidColorBrush foregroundBrush = LoggingBrushes[(int)level];
122. 
123.                 if (IsLogViewFull)
124.                 {
125.                     // Once the view is full, messages go only to the debug console.
126.                     System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine(logEntry);
127.                     return;
128.                 }
129. 
130.                 if (LoggingListBox.Items.Count >= MaxItemsInLogView)
131.                 {
132.                     IsLogViewFull = true;
133. 
134.                     System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine(logEntry);
135.                     logEntry = "Log window is full";
136.                     foregroundBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
137.                 }
138. 
139.                 LoggingListBox.Items.Insert(0, new TextBlock() { Text = logEntry, Foreground = foregroundBrush });
140.             }
141.             else if (level != LogViewLoggingLevel.Verbose)
142.             {
143.                 // Non-Verbose messages blocked from the LogView are shown on the debug console.
144.                 System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"{level}: {logEntry}");
145.             }
146.         }
147.

152.             Clipboard.SetContent(content);
153.         }

implement navigation:\n```csharp\nusing System;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Navigation;\nusing AdaptiveStreaming.Models;\n\nnamespace AdaptiveStreaming;\n\npublic sealed partial class MainPage : Page\n{\n    public static MainPage? Current { get; private set; }\n\n    private readonly Dictionary<string, Type> _pageTypeMap = new()\n    {\n        { \"Scenario1_SimplestAdaptiveStreaming\", typeof(Scenario1_SimplestAdaptiveStreaming) },\n        { \"Scenario2_EventHandlers\", typeof(Scenario2_EventHandlers) },\n        { \"Scenario3_RequestModification\", typeof(Scenario3_RequestModification) },\n        { \"Scenario4_Tuning\", typeof(Scenario4_Tuning) },\n        { \"Scenario5_Metadata\", typeof(Scenario5_Metadata) },\n        { \"Scenario6_AdInsertion\", typeof(Scenario6_AdInsertion) },\n        { \"Scenario7_LiveSeekableRange\", typeof(Scenario7_LiveSeekableRange) },\n    };\n\n    public MainPage()\n    {\n        InitializeComponent();\n        Current = this;\n        // Navigate to first scenario by default\n        NavView.SelectedItem = NavView.MenuItems[0];\n    }\n\n    private void NavView_SelectionChanged(NavigationView sender, NavigationViewSelectionChangedEventArgs args)\n    {\n        if (args.SelectedItem is NavigationViewItem item && item.Tag is string tag)\n        {\n            if (_pageTypeMap.TryGetValue(tag, out var pageType))\n            {\n                ScenarioFrame.Navigate(pageType);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static IReadOnlyList<AdaptiveContentModel> ContentManagementSystemStub =>\n        SampleConfiguration.ContentManagementSystemStub;\n\n    public static AdaptiveContentModel FindContentById(int id) =>\n        SampleConfiguration.FindContentById(id);\n}\n```\n\n**IMPORTANT**: The `SampleConfiguration.cs` file has the static property `ContentManagementSystemStub` and `FindContentById`. Check whether `MainPage.FindContentById()` is referenced in scenario pages, and if so, either keep it as a passthrough or update the scenario pages to reference `SampleConfiguration` directly. Make sure the build compiles.\n\n### 5. Fix SampleConfiguration.cs\n\nThe current `SampleConfiguration.cs` uses `SDKTemplate` namespace. After the namespace fix in step 2, it'll be `AdaptiveStreaming`. But it also currently defines `MainPage` as a partial class. Since we're implementing `MainPage` in `MainPage.xaml.cs`, we need to:\n\n1. Move `ContentManagementSystemStub` and `FindContentById` to NOT be on `MainPage` - instead put them in `SampleConfiguration` as static members (they already reference `AdaptiveContentModel.GetKnownAzureMediaServicesModels()`)\n2. Move `Scenario` class out of the `MainPage` partial if needed\n3. Keep `BitrateHelper` class\n\nThe current `SampleConfiguration.cs` content references `MainPage.scenarios` in a partial class pattern. Since our new `MainPage.xaml.cs` is `sealed partial`, we should remove the `partial class MainPage` section from `SampleConfiguration.cs` and instead make `SampleConfiguration` a standalone class.\n\nHere's the new `SampleConfiguration.cs` content:\n```csharp\nusing AdaptiveStreaming.Models;\nusing System;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing System.Linq;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;\n\nnamespace AdaptiveStreaming\n{\n    public static class SampleConfiguration\n    {\n        public const string FEATURE_NAME = \"AdaptiveStreaming\";\n\n        public static IReadOnlyList<AdaptiveContentModel> ContentManagementSystemStub = \n            AdaptiveContentModel.GetKnownAzureMediaServicesModels();\n        \n        public static AdaptiveContentModel FindContentById(int id)\n        {\n            return ContentManagementSystemStub.First(model => model.Id == id);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public class Scenario\n    {\n        public string Title { get; set; } = string.Empty;\n        public Type ClassType { get; set; } = typeof(Page);\n    }\n\n    public class BitrateHelper\n    {\n        // Each bitrate is assigned a symbol as follows:\n        // The bottom quartile gets 1 bar, the second quartile gets 2 bars,\n        // the third quartile gets 3 bars, and the top quartile gets 4 bars.\n        private Dictionary<uint, Symbol> symbolAssignment = new Dictionary<uint, Symbol>();\n\n        public BitrateHelper(IEnumerable<uint> availableBitRates)\n        {\n            uint[] sortedBitRates = availableBitRates.ToArray();\n            Array.Sort(sortedBitRates);\n            int i = 0;\n            while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 1 / 4)\n            {\n                symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.OneBar);\n            }\n            while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 2 / 4)\n            {\n                symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.TwoBars);\n            }\n            while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 3 / 4)\n            {\n                symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.ThreeBars);\n            }\n            while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 4 / 4)\n            {\n                symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.FourBars);\n            }\n        }\n\n        public Symbol GetBitrateSymbol(uint currentBitrate)\n        {\n            Symbol symbol = Symbol.ZeroBars;\n            if (symbolAssignment.TryGetValue(currentBitrate, out symbol))\n            {\n                return symbol;\n            }\n            return Symbol.ZeroBars;\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 6. After our changes, `MainPage.Current` still exists and `MainPage.FindContentById` is a passthrough to `SampleConfiguration.FindContentById`. Add these to the `.csproj` as excluded:\n\nIn `AdaptiveStreaming.csproj`, add:\n```xml\n<ItemGroup>\n    <Compile Remove=\"bin\\**\" />\n    <Compile Remove=\"obj\\**\" />\n    <None Include=\"bin\\**\" />\n    <None Include=\"obj\\**\" />\n</ItemGroup>\n```\n\nActually, the `bin` and `obj` folders are typically excluded automatically. Common issues:\n- `CoreDispatcherPriority` still referenced: remove `using Windows.UI.Core`\n- `Dispatcher.RunAsync` still referenced: replace with `DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue`  \n- Missing `using` statements: add as needed\n- Type not found: check namespace issues\n- `Windows.UI.Colors` (from LogView.xaml.cs): replace with `Microsoft.UI` or use `Windows.UI.Color` directly (Windows.UI.Colors is still available in WinUI 3 from the Windows SDK)\n\n### 11. change to `DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(ClearLog)`\n\n## Threading Pattern Summary\n\nUWP pattern (to remove):\n```csharp\nawait this.Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => { ... });\n```\nWinUI 3 (replacement):\n```csharp\nDispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() => { ... });\n```\n\nFor `var task = Dispatcher.RunAsync(...)` (without await):\n```csharp\nvar task = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => { ... });\n```\nReplace with:\n```csharp\nDispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() => { ... });\n```\n\nFor `Dispatcher.HasThreadAccess` check:\n```csharp\nif (Dispatcher.HasThreadAccess) { ... } else { await Dispatcher.RunAsync(..., ...) }\n```\nReplace with:\n```csharp\nif (DispatcherQueue.HasThreadAccess) { ... } else { DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(...) }\n```\n\n## After Validation Passes\n\nRun the test launch:\n```powershell\n& \"C:\\ado\\win-dev-skills-benchmark\\agent-benchmark\\results\\run5\\000_AdaptiveStreaming\\app\\.github\\skills\\winui-uwp-migration\\scripts\\Test-AppLaunch.ps1\" -Target \"C:\\ado\\win-dev-skills-benchmark\\agent-benchmark\\results\\run5\\000_AdaptiveStreaming\\app\\AdaptiveStreaming\"\n```\n\nFix any startup crashes.\n"}

//*********************************************************using System;
using System.Linq;
using Windows.ApplicationModel.DataTransfer;
using Windows.UI;
using Windows.UI.Core;
using Windows.UI.Xaml;
using Windows.UI.Xaml.Controls;
using Windows.UI.Xaml.Media;namespace SDKTemplate.Logging
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Logging levels supported by the LogView control.
    /// The "Always" level is supported only by the Log method.
    /// </summary>
    public enum LogViewLoggingLevel
    {
        Verbose = 0,
        Information,
        Warning,
        Error,
        Critical,
        Always,
    }    /// 
    /// <summary>
    /// This control can be used to log events from a scenario page.
    /// 
    /// In the XAML markup, you can set the initial logging level by setting
    /// LoggingLevel="Verbose", or any other enum value of LogViewLoggingLevel.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed partial class LogView : UserControl
    {
        public LogView()
        {
            this.InitializeComponent();            LoggingBrushes = new SolidColorBrush[] {
                (SolidColorBrush)Resources["VerboseLogBrush"],
                (SolidColorBrush)Resources["InformationLogBrush"],
                (SolidColorBrush)Resources["WarningLogBrush"],
                (SolidColorBrush)Resources["ErrorLogBrush"],
                (SolidColorBrush)Resources["CriticalLogBrush"],
                (SolidColorBrush)Resources["AlwaysLogBrush"],
            };            LogLevelComboBox.SelectedIndex = (int)LoggingLevel;
        }        public static readonly DependencyProperty LogLevelProperty =
            DependencyProperty.Register("LoggingLevel", typeof(LogViewLoggingLevel), typeof(LogView),
                new PropertyMetadata(LogViewLoggingLevel.Warning, OnLoggingLevelChanged));        public LogViewLoggingLevel LoggingLevel
        {
            get { return (LogViewLoggingLevel)GetValue(LogLevelProperty); }
            set { SetValue(LogLevelProperty, value); }
        }

        private static void OnLoggingLevelChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            var logger = (LogView)d;
            var loggingLevel = (LogViewLoggingLevel)e.NewValue;
            logger.LogLevelComboBox.SelectedIndex = (int)loggingLevel;
        }

        bool IsLogViewFull = false;
        int MaxItemsInLogView = 500;

        SolidColorBrush[] LoggingBrushes;

        /// <summary>
        /// Changes the logging level at which messages are filtered.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="sender"></param>
        /// <param name="e"></param>
        private void LogLevel_Changed(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            LoggingLevel = (LogViewLoggingLevel)LogLevelComboBox.SelectedIndex;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Provides logging with filtering based on UI settings.
        /// Logging functions tangential to main scenarios should specify a log view level less than Always.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="message">message to be logged</param>
        /// <param name="level">message category</param>
        public async void Log(string message, LogViewLoggingLevel level = LogViewLoggingLevel.Always)
        {
            if (Dispatcher.HasThreadAccess)
            {
                LogFromUIThread(message, level);
            }
            else
            {
                await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
                {
                    LogFromUIThread(message, level);
                });
            }
        }

        private void LogFromUIThread(string message, LogViewLoggingLevel level)
        {
            string logEntry = $"{DateTime.Now:HH:mm:ss.fff} - {message}";

            if (LoggingLevel <= level)
            {
                SolidColorBrush foregroundBrush = LoggingBrushes[(int)level];

                if (IsLogViewFull)
                {
                    // Once the view is full, messages go only to the debug console.
                    System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine(logEntry);
                    return;
                }

                if (LoggingListBox.Items.Count >= MaxItemsInLogView)
                {
                    IsLogViewFull = true;

                    System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine(logEntry);
                    logEntry = "Log window is full";
                    foregroundBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);
                }

                LoggingListBox.Items.Insert(0, new TextBlock() { Text = logEntry, Foreground = foregroundBrush });      
            }
            else if (level != LogViewLoggingLevel.Verbose)
            {
                // Non-Verbose messages blocked from the LogView are shown on the debug console.
                System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"{level}: {logEntry}");
            }
        }

        private void CopyLogToClipboard_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            var content = new DataPackage();
            content.SetText(String.Join("\r\n", LoggingListBox.Items.Select(item => (item as TextBlock).Text)));        
            Clipboard.SetContent(content);
        }

        private void ClearLog_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            ClearLog();
        }

        public async void ClearLog()
        {
            if (Dispatcher.HasThreadAccess)
            {
                LoggingListBox.Items.Clear();
                IsLogViewFull = false;
            }
            else
            {
                await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, ClearLog);
            }
        }
    }
}

//*********************************************************using SDKTemplate.Logging.Extensions;
using System;
using Windows.Media.Streaming.Adaptive;namespace SDKTemplate.Logging
{
    public class AdaptiveMediaSourceLogger : IDisposable
    {
        #region Common pattern for all loggers        private LogView logView;
        private AdaptiveMediaSource adaptiveMediaSource;        public AdaptiveMediaSourceLogger(LogView _logView, AdaptiveMediaSource _adaptiveMediaSource)
        {
            if (_logView == null)
                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(_logView));
            this.logView = _logView;            if (_adaptiveMediaSource == null)
                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(_adaptiveMediaSource));
            this.adaptiveMediaSource = _adaptiveMediaSource;            this.RegisterForAdaptiveMediaSourceEvents();
        }        public void Dispose()
        {
            this.UnregisterForAdaptiveMediaSourceEvents();            this.logView = null;
            this.adaptiveMediaSource = null;
        }
        
        #endregion        #region AdaptiveMediaSource Event Handlers        private void RegisterForAdaptiveMediaSourceEvents()
        {
            adaptiveMediaSource.Diagnostics.DiagnosticAvailable += DiagnosticAvailable;
            adaptiveMediaSource.DownloadBitrateChanged += DownloadBitrateChanged;
            adaptiveMediaSource.DownloadCompleted += DownloadCompleted;
            adaptiveMediaSource.DownloadFailed += DownloadFailed;
            adaptiveMediaSource.DownloadRequested += DownloadRequested;
            adaptiveMediaSource.PlaybackBitrateChanged += PlaybackBitrateChanged;
        }        private void UnregisterForAdaptiveMediaSourceEvents()
        {
            if (adaptiveMediaSource == null)
            {
                return;
            }
            adaptiveMediaSource.Diagnostics.DiagnosticAvailable -= DiagnosticAvailable;
            adaptiveMediaSource.DownloadBitrateChanged -= DownloadBitrateChanged;
            adaptiveMediaSource.DownloadCompleted -= DownloadCompleted;
            adaptiveMediaSource.DownloadFailed -= DownloadFailed;
            adaptiveMediaSource.DownloadRequested -= DownloadRequested;
            adaptiveMediaSource.PlaybackBitrateChanged -= PlaybackBitrateChanged;
        }

        private void DiagnosticAvailable(AdaptiveMediaSourceDiagnostics sender, AdaptiveMediaSourceDiagnosticAvailableEv
ventArgs args)
        {
            logView.Log($"{args.ToJsonObject()}", LogViewLoggingLevel.Warning);
        }

        private void DownloadBitrateChanged(AdaptiveMediaSource sender, AdaptiveMediaSourceDownloadBitrateChangedEventAr
rgs args)
        {
            logView.Log($"{args.ToJsonObject()}", LogViewLoggingLevel.Information);
        }

        private void DownloadCompleted(AdaptiveMediaSource sender, AdaptiveMediaSourceDownloadCompletedEventArgs args)  
        {
            logView.Log($"{args.ToJsonObject()}", LogViewLoggingLevel.Verbose);
        }

        private void DownloadFailed(AdaptiveMediaSource sender, AdaptiveMediaSourceDownloadFailedEventArgs args)        
        {
            logView.Log($"{args.ToJsonObject()}", LogViewLoggingLevel.Error);
        }

        private void DownloadRequested(AdaptiveMediaSource sender, AdaptiveMediaSourceDownloadRequestedEventArgs args)  
        {
            logView.Log($"{args.ToJsonObject()}", LogViewLoggingLevel.Verbose);
        }

        private void PlaybackBitrateChanged(AdaptiveMediaSource sender, AdaptiveMediaSourcePlaybackBitrateChangedEventAr
rgs args)
        {
            logView.Log($"{args.ToJsonObject()}", LogViewLoggingLevel.Information);
        }
        #endregion

    }
}

//*********************************************************using SDKTemplate.Logging.Extensions;
using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Linq;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices.WindowsRuntime;
using Windows.Foundation;
using Windows.Web.Http;
using Windows.Web.Http.Filters;namespace SDKTemplate.Logging
{
    public class AdaptiveMediaSourceHttpFilterLogger : IHttpFilter
    {
        #region Common pattern for all loggers        private LogView logView;
        private IHttpFilter innerFilter;
        private Stopwatch timeToResponseComplete;        public AdaptiveMediaSourceHttpFilterLogger(LogView _logView, IHttpFilter _innerFilter)
        {
            if (_logView == null)
                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(_logView));
            this.logView = _logView;            if (_innerFilter == null)
                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(_innerFilter));
            this.innerFilter = _innerFilter;            this.timeToResponseComplete = new Stopwatch();
        }        public void Dispose()
        {            this.timeToResponseComplete = null;
            this.logView = null;
            this.innerFilter = null;
        }
        
        #endregion        public IAsyncOperationWithProgress<HttpResponseMessage, HttpProgress> SendRequestAsync(HttpRequestMessage request)
        {
            timeToResponseComplete.Start();
            logView.Log($"{{\"FILTER SendRequestAsync\":{{\"requestUri\":\"{request.RequestUri}\"}}}}", LogViewLoggingLevel.Verbose);            return AsyncInfo.Run<HttpResponseMessage, HttpProgress>(async (cancellationToken, operationProgressReporter) =>
            {
                IAsyncOperationWithProgress<HttpResponseMessage, HttpProgress> innerOperation = innerFilter.SendRequestAsync(request);                innerOperation.Progress += new AsyncOperationProgressHandler<HttpResponseMessage, HttpProgress>(
                    (IAsyncOperationWithProgress<HttpResponseMessage, HttpProgress> asyncInfo, HttpProgress httpProgress
sInfo) =>
                {
                    logView.Log($"{{\"FILTER Progress Handler\": {{\"requestUri\":\"{request.RequestUri}\", {httpProgres
ssInfo.ToJsonObject()}}}}}", LogViewLoggingLevel.Verbose);
                });

                HttpResponseMessage response = await innerOperation.AsTask(cancellationToken, operationProgressReporter)
);
                cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
                // At this point, SendRequest has completed and we have a response.
                timeToResponseComplete.Stop();

                // We could inspect the content of the response headers:
                var countHeaders = response.Headers.Count();
                logView.Log($"{{\"FILTER Response Complete\": {{\"StatusCode\":\"{response.StatusCode}\",\"timeToRespons
seComplete\":\"{timeToResponseComplete.ElapsedMilliseconds}\",\"requestUri\":\"{request.RequestUri}\",\"countHeaders\":\"
"{countHeaders}\"}}}}", LogViewLoggingLevel.Verbose);

                if(!response.IsSuccessStatusCode)
                {
                    logView.Log($"{{\"FILTER Response Failed\": {{\"StatusCode\":\"{response.StatusCode}\",\"timeToRespo
onseComplete\":\"{timeToResponseComplete.ElapsedMilliseconds}\",\"requestUri\":\"{request.RequestUri}\",\"countHeaders\":
:\"{countHeaders}\"}}}}", LogViewLoggingLevel.Error);
                }

                // Note that we should NOT attempt to access the response.Content in an IHttpFilter such as this,       
                // the Content stream is for the base filter and AdaptiveMediaSource to use.
                //
                // If you want to download content in the App and then pass it to the AdaptiveMediaSource,
                // see AppDownloadedKeyRequest in Scenario3, which uses Deferral objects to manage thread timing        
                // with the AdaptiveMediaSource.
                return response;
            });
        }

    }
}

205.         private void InitializeBitrateLists(AdaptiveMediaSource aMS)
206.         {
207.             var sortedBitrates = aMS.AvailableBitrates.OrderByDescending(br => br).Select(br => new BitrateItem(br)).ToArray();
208.             InitialBitrateList.ItemsSource = sortedBitrates;
209.             var selected = sortedBitrates.First(item => item.Bitrate == aMS.InitialBitrate);
210.             InitialBitrateList.SelectedItem = sortedBitrates.FirstOrDefault(item => item.Bitrate == aMS.InitialBitrate);
211. 
212.             var nullableSortedBitrates = (new BitrateItem[] { new BitrateItem(null) }).Concat(sortedBitrates).ToArray();
213.             DesiredMaxBitrateList.ItemsSource = DesiredMinBitrateList.ItemsSource = nullableSortedBitrates;
214. 
215.             DesiredMaxBitrateList.SelectedItem = nullableSortedBitrates.First(item => item.Bitrate == aMS.DesiredMaxBitrate);
216.             DesiredMinBitrateList.SelectedItem = nullableSortedBitrates.First(item => item.Bitrate == aMS.DesiredMinBitrate);
217.         }
218. 
219.         // An argument exception will be thrown if the following constraint is not met:
220.         //   DesiredMinBitrate <= InitialBitrate <= DesiredMaxBitrate
221.         private bool IsValidBitrateCombination(uint? desiredMinBitrate, uint? desiredMaxBitrate, uint initialBitrate)
222.         {
223.             // The ">" operator returns false if either operand is null. We take advantage of this
224.             // by testing in this manner. Do NOT "optimize" this by reversing the sense of the test
225.             // to "return desiredMinBitrate <= initialBitrate && initialBitrate <= desiredMaxBitrate;"
226.             if (desiredMinBitrate > initialBitrate || initialBitrate > desiredMaxBitrate)
227.             {
228.                 return false;
229.             }
230.             return true;
231.         }
232. 
233.         private void InitialBitrateList_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
234.         {
235.             var selection = (e.AddedItems.Count > 0) ? (BitrateItem)e.AddedItems[0] : null;
236.             if (selection != null && adaptiveMediaSource.InitialBitrate != selection.Bitrate)
237.             {
238.                 if (IsValidBitrateCombination(adaptiveMediaSource.DesiredMinBitrate, adaptiveMediaSource.DesiredMaxBitrate, selection.Bitrate.Value))
239.                 {
240.                     adaptiveMediaSource.InitialBitrate = selection.Bitrate.Value;
241.                 }
242.                 else
243.                 {
244.                     InitialBitrateList.SelectedItem = e.RemovedItems[0];
245.                 }
246.             }
247.         }
248. 
249.         private void DesiredMinBitrateList_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
250.         {
251.             var selection = (e.AddedItems.Count > 0) ? (BitrateItem)e.AddedItems[0] : null;
252.             if (selection != null && adaptiveMediaSource.DesiredMinBitrate != selection.Bitrate)
253.             {
254.                 if (IsValidBitrateCombination(selection.Bitrate, adaptiveMediaSource.DesiredMaxBitrate, adaptiveMediaSource.InitialBitrate))
255.                 {
256.                     adaptiveMediaSource.DesiredMinBitrate = selection.Bitrate;
257.                 }
258.                 else
259.                 {
260.                     DesiredMinBitrateList.SelectedItem = e.RemovedItems[0];
261.                 }
262.             }
263.         }
264. 
265.         private void DesiredMaxBitrateList_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
266.         {
267.             var selection = (e.AddedItems.Count > 0) ? (BitrateItem)e.AddedItems[0] : null;
268.             if (selection != null && adaptiveMediaSource.DesiredMaxBitrate != selection.Bitrate)
269.             {
270.                 if (IsValidBitrateCombination(adaptiveMediaSource.DesiredMinBitrate, selection.Bitrate, adaptiveMediaSource.InitialBitrate))
271.                 {
272.                     adaptiveMediaSource.DesiredMaxBitrate = selection.Bitrate;
273.                 }
274.                 else
275.                 {
276.                     DesiredMaxBitrateList.SelectedItem = e.RemovedItems[0];
277.                 }
278.             }
279.         }

181.         private void RegisterForMediaSourceEvents(MediaSource source)
182.         {
183.             source.OpenOperationCompleted += Source_OpenOperationCompleted;
184.         }

189. 
190.         private void Source_OpenOperationCompleted(MediaSource source, MediaSourceOpenOperationCompletedEventArgs args)
191.         {
192.             // Here we create our own metadata track.
193.             // Our goal is to track progress through the asset and report it back to a server.
194.             object customValue = null;
195.             source.CustomProperties.TryGetValue("contentId", out customValue);
196.             string contentId = (string)customValue;
197.             var reportingUriFormat = "http://myserver/reporting/{0}?id={1}";
198.             // From \Shared\TrackingEventCue.cs :
199.             TrackingEventCue.CreateTrackingEventsTrack(source, contentId, reportingUriFormat);
200.         }
201. 
202.         #endregion
203. 
204. 
205.         #region MediaPlaybackItem Event Handlers
206. 
207.         private void RegisterForMediaPlaybackItemEvents(MediaPlaybackItem item)
208.         {
209.             // If we encountered some unknown tags in an m3u8 manifest,
210.             // we might already have metadata tracks in the source,
211.             // which is used as the constructor for the MediaPlaybackItem.
212.             // Therefore, the MediaPlaybackItem may already have metadata tracks.
213.             // Since TracksChanged will not be raised for these it is best to check
214.             // if we have tracks, and set up the handlers:
215.             if (item.TimedMetadataTracks.Count > 0)
216.             {
217.                 Log($"Registering existing TimedMetadataTracks, Count: {item.TimedMetadataTracks.Count}");
218. 
219.                 for (int index = 0; index < item.TimedMetadataTracks.Count; index++)
220.                 {
221.                     RegisterMetadataHandlers(item, index);
222.                 }
223.             }
224. 
225.             // For any tracks found as the content is parsed, we register for the event handler:
226.             item.TimedMetadataTracksChanged += Item_TimedMetadataTracksChanged;
227.         }
228. 
229.         private void UnregisterForMediaPlaybackItemEvents(MediaPlaybackItem item)
230.         {
231.             item.TimedMetadataTracksChanged -= Item_TimedMetadataTracksChanged;
232.             foreach (var track in item.TimedMetadataTracks)
233.             {
234.                 UnregisterMetadataHandlers(track);
235.             }
236.         }
237. 
238.         private void Item_TimedMetadataTracksChanged(MediaPlaybackItem item, IVectorChangedEventArgs args)
239.         {
240.             Log($"item.TimedMetadataTracksChanged: CollectionChange:{args.CollectionChange} Index:{args.Index} Total:{item.TimedMetadataTracks.Count}");
241. 
242.             if (args.CollectionChange == CollectionChange.ItemInserted)
243.             {
244.                 RegisterMetadataHandlers(item, (int)args.Index);
245.             }
246.             if (args.CollectionChange == CollectionChange.Reset)
247.             {
248.                 for (int index = 0; index < item.TimedMetadataTracks.Count; index++)
249.                 {
250.                     RegisterMetadataHandlers(item, index);
251.                 }
252.             }
253.         }
254. 
255.         private void RegisterMetadataHandlers(MediaPlaybackItem mediaPlaybackItem, int index)
256.         {
257.             var timedTrack = mediaPlaybackItem.TimedMetadataTracks[index];
258.             object customValue = null;
259.             mediaPlaybackItem.Source.CustomProperties.TryGetValue("contentId", out customValue);
260.             string contentId = (string)customValue;
261. 
262.             StringBuilder logMsg = new StringBuilder($"{contentId} ");
263. 
264.             if (timedTrack.TrackKind == MediaTrackKind.TimedMetadata &&
265.                 (timedTrack.TimedMetadataKind == TimedMetadataKind.Data ||
266.                 timedTrack.TimedMetadataKind == TimedMetadataKind.Custom))
267.             {
268.                 // Check for known dispatchTypes:
269.                 var dispatchType = mediaPlaybackItem.TimedMetadataTracks[index].DispatchType;
270.                 if (String.Equals(dispatchType, "EXTM3U", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
271.                 {
272.                     timedTrack.CueEntered += metadata_extm3u_CueEntered;
273.                     timedTrack.Label = "HLS Manifest comments";
274.                     // Tell the platform that the app will render these.
275.                     // Without this, the CueEntered event will not be raised by the platform.
276.                     mediaPlaybackItem.TimedMetadataTracks.SetPresentationMode((uint)index, TimedMetadataTrackPresentationMode.ApplicationPresented);
277.                 }
278.                 else if (string.Equals(dispatchType, "15260DFFFF49443320FF49443320000F", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) // per Apple TS spec for ID3 meta
279.                 {
280.                     timedTrack.CueEntered += metadata_id3_CueEntered;
281.                     timedTrack.Label = "ID3 Tags";
282.                     mediaPlaybackItem.TimedMetadataTracks.SetPresentationMode((uint)index, TimedMetadataTrackPresentationMode.ApplicationPresented);
283.                 }
284.                 else if (string.Equals(dispatchType, "emsg:mp4", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) // For 'emsg' box embedded in mp4 container
285.                 {
286.                     timedTrack.CueEntered += metadata_emsg_mp4_CueEntered;
287.                     timedTrack.Label = "emsg payload";
288.                     mediaPlaybackItem.TimedMetadataTracks.SetPresentationMode((uint)index, TimedMetadataTrackPresentationMode.ApplicationPresented);
289.                 }
290. 
291.                 // Check for our Custom TimedMetadataTrack
292.                 if (mediaPlaybackItem.TimedMetadataTracks[index].Id == "TrackingEvents")
293.                 {
294.                     timedTrack.CueEntered += metadata_TrackingEvents_CueEntered;
295.                     timedTrack.CueExited += metadata_TrackingEvents_CueExited;
296.                     timedTrack.Label = "Tracking Events";
297.                     mediaPlaybackItem.TimedMetadataTracks.SetPresentationMode((uint)index, TimedMetadataTrackPresentationMode.ApplicationPresented);
298.                 }
299. 
300.                 logMsg.AppendLine($"Registered CueEntered for {timedTrack.Label}.");
301.             }
302.             else
303.             {

334.         {
335.             StringBuilder logMsg = new StringBuilder();
336. 
337.             object customValue = null;
338.             timedMetadataTrack.PlaybackItem.Source.CustomProperties.TryGetValue("contentId", out customValue);
339.             string contentId = (string)customValue;
340.             logMsg.AppendLine($"{contentId} M3U8 DataCue CueEntered raised.");
341.             var dataCue = args.Cue as DataCue;
342.             if (dataCue != null && dataCue.Data != null)
343.             {
344.                 // The payload is a UTF-16 Little Endian null-terminated string.
345.                 // It is any comment line in a manifest that is not part of the HLS spec.

382.         private UInt32 lastProcessedAdId = 0;
383.         private void metadata_emsg_mp4_CueEntered(TimedMetadataTrack timedMetadataTrack, MediaCueEventArgs args)
384.         {
385.             StringBuilder logMsg = new StringBuilder();
386.             object customValue = null;
387.             timedMetadataTrack.PlaybackItem.Source.CustomProperties.TryGetValue("contentId", out customValue);
388.             string contentId = (string)customValue;
389.             logMsg.AppendLine($"{contentId} mp4 emsg DataCue CueEntered raised.");
390.             var dataCue = args.Cue as DataCue;
391.             if (dataCue != null)
392.             {
393.                 // Ref: ISO/IEC 20009-1, Section 5.10.3.3.2 Definition of Box Type: 'emsg'
394.                 string scheme_id_uri = string.Empty;
395.                 string value = string.Empty;
396.                 UInt32 timescale = (UInt32) TimeSpan.TicksPerSecond;
397.                 UInt32 presentation_time_delta = (UInt32)dataCue.StartTime.Ticks;
398.                 UInt32 event_duration = (UInt32)dataCue.Duration.Ticks;
399.                 UInt32 id = 0;
400.                 Byte[] message_data = null;
401. 
402.                 const string scheme_id_uri_key = "emsg:scheme_id_uri";
403.                 object propValue = null;
404.                 dataCue.Properties.TryGetValue(scheme_id_uri_key, out propValue);
405.                 scheme_id_uri = propValue!=null ? (string)propValue : "";
406. 
407.                 const string value_key = "emsg:value";
408.                 propValue = null;
409.                 dataCue.Properties.TryGetValue(value_key, out propValue);
410.                 value = propValue != null ? (string)propValue : "";
411. 
412.                 const string timescale_key = "emsg:timescale";
413.                 propValue = null;
414.                 dataCue.Properties.TryGetValue(timescale_key, out propValue);
415.                 timescale = propValue != null ? (UInt32)propValue : timescale;
416. 
417.                 const string presentation_time_delta_key = "emsg:presentation_time_delta";
418.                 propValue = null;
419.                 dataCue.Properties.TryGetValue(presentation_time_delta_key, out propValue);
420.                 presentation_time_delta = propValue != null ? (UInt32)propValue : presentation_time_delta;
421. 
422.                 const string event_duration_key = "emsg:event_duration";
423.                 propValue = null;
424.                 dataCue.Properties.TryGetValue(event_duration_key, out propValue);
425.                 event_duration = propValue != null ? (UInt32)propValue : event_duration;
426. 
427.                 const string id_key = "emsg:id";
428.                 propValue = null;
429.                 dataCue.Properties.TryGetValue(id_key, out propValue);
430.                 id = propValue != null ? (UInt32)propValue : 0;
431. 
432.                 logMsg.AppendLine($"Label: {timedMetadataTrack.Label}, Id: {dataCue.Id}, StartTime: {dataCue.StartTime}, Duration: {dataCue.Duration}");
433.                 logMsg.Append($"scheme_id_uri: {scheme_id_uri}, value: {value}, timescale: {timescale}, presentation_time_delta: {presentation_time_delta}, event_duration: {event_duration}, id: {id}");
434. 
435.                 if (dataCue.Data != null)
436.                 {
437.                     var dr = DataReader.FromBuffer(dataCue.Data);
438. 
439.                     // Check if this is a SCTE ad message:
440.                     // Ref:  http://dashif.org/identifiers/event-schemes/
441.                     if (scheme_id_uri.ToLower() == "urn:scte:scte35:2013:xml")
442.                     {
443.                         // SCTE recommends publishing emsg more than once, so we avoid reprocessing the same message id:
444.                         if (lastProcessedAdId != id)
445.                         {
446.                             lastProcessedAdId = id; // TODO: Requires robustness. Messages may be out of order, which would break this sample's logic.
447. 
448.                             dr.UnicodeEncoding = Windows.Storage.Streams.UnicodeEncoding.Utf8;
449.                             var scte35payload = dr.ReadString(dataCue.Data.Length);
450.                             logMsg.Append($", message_data: {scte35payload}");
451.                             ScheduleAdFromScte35Payload(timedMetadataTrack, presentation_time_delta, timescale, event_duration, scte35payload);
452.                         }
453.                         else
454.                         {
455.                             logMsg.AppendLine($"This emsg.Id, {id}, has already been processed.");
456.                         }
457.                     }
458.                     else
459.                     {
460.                         message_data = new byte[dataCue.Data.Length];
461.                         dr.ReadBytes(message_data);
462.                         // TODO: Use the 'emsg' bytes for something useful.
463.                         logMsg.Append($", message_data.Length: {message_data.Length}");
464.                     }
465.                 }
466.             }
467.             Log(logMsg.ToString());
468.         }

470.         /// <summary>
471.         /// This function attempts to parse the SCTE-35 payload, then schedule an appropriately offset Ad using the MediaBreak APIs
472.         /// </summary>
473.         /// <param name="timedMetadataTrack">Track which fired the cue</param>
474.         /// <param name="presentation_time_delta"></param>
475.         /// <param name="timescale"></param>
476.         /// <param name="event_duration"></param>
477.         /// <param name="scte35payload"></param>
478.         private async void ScheduleAdFromScte35Payload(TimedMetadataTrack timedMetadataTrack, uint presentation_time_delta, uint timescale, uint event_duration, string scte35payload)
479.         {
480.             // TODO: Parse SCTE-35
481.             //
482.             // Ref:
483.             // http://www.scte.org/SCTEDocs/Standards/ANSI_SCTE%20214-3%202015.pdf
484.             //
485.             // Eg:
486.             //
487.             // <SpliceInfoSection ptsAdjustment="0" scte35:tier="4095">
488.             //  <SpliceInsert spliceEventId="147467497"
489.             //                spliceEventCancelIndicator="false"
490.             //                outOfNetworkIndicator="false"
491.             //                uniqueProgramId="0"
492.             //                availNum="0"
493.             //                availsExpected="0"
494.             //                spliceImmediateFlag="false" >
495.             //    <Program>
496.             //      <SpliceTime ptsTime="6257853600"/>
497.             //    </Program>
498.             //    <BreakDuration autoReturn="true" duration="900000"/>
499.             //  </SpliceInsert>
500.             // </SpliceInfoSection>
501.             //
502.             //
503. 
504.             // We use the metadata track object which fired the Cue to walk our way back up the
505.             // media object stack to find our original AdaptiveMediaSource.
506.             //
507.             // The AdaptiveMediaSource is required because the SCTE-35 payload includes
508.             // timing offsets which are relative to the original content PTS -- see below.
509.             var ams = timedMetadataTrack.PlaybackItem.Source.AdaptiveMediaSource;
510. 
511.             if (ams != null && timescale != 0)
512.             {
513.                 // ++++
514.                 // NOTE: DO NOT PARSE SCTE35 LIKE THIS IN PRODUCTION CODE!
515.                 //
516.                 // Reminder:
517.                 //
518.                 // THIS CODE IS PROVIDED *AS IS* WITHOUT WARRANTY OF
519.                 // ANY KIND, EITHER EXPRESS OR IMPLIED, INCLUDING ANY
520.                 // IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR
521.                 // PURPOSE, MERCHANTABILITY, OR NON-INFRINGEMENT.
522.                 //
523.                 // We will not demonstrate proper SCTE35 parsing in this sample,
524.                 // but instead we search through the xml find the ptsTime if present,
525.                 // and use it to schedule an ad; keeping in place the various time offsets.
526.                 //
527.                 // e.g.: var sampleScte35Payload = "<SpliceInfoSection ptsAdjustment=\"0\" scte35:tier=\"4095\">< SpliceInsert spliceEventId = \"147467497\" spliceEventCancelIndicator = \"false\" outOfNetworkIndicator = \"false\" uniqueProgramId = \"0\" availNum = \"0\" availsExpected = \"0\" spliceImmediateFlag = \"false\" > < Program >< SpliceTime ptsTime = \"6257853600\" /></ Program >  < BreakDuration autoReturn = \"true\" duration = \"900000\" />  </ SpliceInsert > </ SpliceInfoSection > ";
528.                 //
529.                 var xmlStrings = scte35payload.Split(new string[] { "<", "/", ">", " ", "=", "\"" }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
530.                 string ptsTime = string.Empty;
531.                 for (int i = 0; i < xmlStrings.Length; i++)
532.                 {
533.                     if (xmlStrings[i] == "ptsTime")
534.                     {
535.                         if (i + 1 < xmlStrings.Length)
536.                         {
537.                             ptsTime = xmlStrings[i + 1];
538.                             break;
539.                         }

543. 
544.                 // The AdaptiveMediaSource keeps track of the original PTS in an AdaptiveMediaSourceCorrelatedTimes
545.                 // object that can be retrieved via ams.GetCorrelatedTimes().  All the while, it provids the
546.                 // media pipeline with a consistent timeline that re-aligns zero at the begining  of the SeekableRange
547.                 // when first joining a Live stream.
548.                 long pts = 0;

AdaptiveContentModel.GetKnownAzureMediaServicesModels();\n        \n        public static AdaptiveContentModel FindContentById(int id)\n        {\n            return ContentManagementSystemStub.First(model => model.Id == id);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public class Scenario\n    {\n        public string Title { get; set; } = string.Empty;\n        public Type ClassType { get; set; } = typeof(Page);\n    }\n\n    public class BitrateHelper\n    {\n        private Dictionary<uint, Symbol> symbolAssignment = new Dictionary<uint, Symbol>();\n\n        public BitrateHelper(IEnumerable<uint> availableBitRates)\n        {\n            uint[] sortedBitRates = availableBitRates.ToArray();\n            Array.Sort(sortedBitRates);\n            int i = 0;\n            while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 1 / 4)\n            {\n                symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.OneBar);\n            }\n            while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 2 / 4)\n            {\n                symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.TwoBars);\n            }\n            while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 3 / 4)\n            {\n                symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.ThreeBars);\n            }\n            while (i < sortedBitRates.Length * 4 / 4)\n            {\n                symbolAssignment.Add(sortedBitRates[i++], Symbol.FourBars);\n            }\n        }\n\n        public Symbol GetBitrateSymbol(uint currentBitrate)\n        {\n            Symbol symbol = Symbol.ZeroBars;\n            if (symbolAssignment.TryGetValue(currentBitrate, out symbol))\n            {\n                return symbol;\n            }\n            return Symbol.ZeroBars;\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## STEP 7: Fix App.xaml.cs\n\nThe App.xaml.cs has many unnecessary usings. 34.         private MediaPlayer mediaPlayer;
35.         private IEnumerable<AdaptiveContentModel> adaptiveContentModels;
36.         public AdaptiveContentModel SelectedModel;
37.         private LogView loggerControl;
38.         Func<Uri, HttpClient, Task<MediaPlaybackItem>> CreateMediaPlaybackItem;
39.         public MediaPlaybackItem MediaPlaybackItem;

41. 
42.         internal void Initialize(MediaPlayer mediaPlayer, 
43.             IEnumerable<AdaptiveContentModel> adaptiveContentModels,
44.             HttpClient optionalHttpClient,
45.             LogView loggerControl, 
46.             Func<Uri, HttpClient, Task<MediaPlaybackItem>> loadSourceFromUriAsync)
47.         {
48.             if (mediaPlayer == null)
49.                 throw new ArgumentNullException("mediaPlayer");
50.             this.mediaPlayer = mediaPlayer;
51. 
52.             if (adaptiveContentModels == null)
53.                 throw new ArgumentNullException("adaptiveContentModels");
54.             this.adaptiveContentModels = adaptiveContentModels;
55. 
56.             if (loggerControl == null)
57.                 throw new ArgumentNullException("loggerControl");
58.             this.loggerControl = loggerControl;
59. 
60.             if (loadSourceFromUriAsync == null)
61.                 throw new ArgumentNullException("loadSourceFromUriAsync");
62.             this.CreateMediaPlaybackItem = loadSourceFromUriAsync;
63. 
64.             this.optionalHttpClient = optionalHttpClient;
65. 
66.             // Set data context:
67.             SelectedContent.ItemsSource = this.adaptiveContentModels;
68.             // Set default item:
69.             SetSelectedModel(this.adaptiveContentModels.First());
70.         }
71. 
72.         public void SetSelectedModel(AdaptiveContentModel model)
73.         {
74.             SelectedModel = model;
75.             SelectedContent.SelectedItem = SelectedModel;
76.         }

80.             // TODO[migrate-001]: see PATTERNS.md#threading

95.         private async void LoadId_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
96.         {
97.             if (mediaPlayer == null || adaptiveContentModels == null || loggerControl == null)
98.                 return;
99. 
100.             this.SelectedModel = (AdaptiveContentModel)SelectedContent.SelectedItem;
101. 
102.             UriBox.Text = SelectedModel.ManifestUri.ToString();
103.             await CreateNewMediaPlaybackItem(SelectedModel.ManifestUri);
104.         }

106.         private async void LoadUri_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
107.         {
108.             if (mediaPlayer == null || adaptiveContentModels == null || loggerControl == null)
109.                 return;
110. 
111.             Uri uri;
112.             if (!Uri.TryCreate(UriBox.Text, UriKind.Absolute, out uri))
113.             {
114.                 loggerControl.Log("Malformed Uri in Load text box.", SDKTemplate.Logging.LogViewLoggingLevel.Critical);
115.                 return;
116.             }
117.             await CreateNewMediaPlaybackItem(uri);
118.         }
119. 
120.         private async Task CreateNewMediaPlaybackItem(Uri uri)
121.         {
122.             SetSourceEnabled(false);
123.             this.MediaPlaybackItem = null;
124.             this.MediaPlaybackItem = await CreateMediaPlaybackItem(uri, optionalHttpClient);
125.             if (this.MediaPlaybackItem != null)
126.             {
127.                 loggerControl.Log($"Loaded Uri: {uri}");
128.                 SetSourceEnabled(true);
129.             }
130.         }

136.             {

138.             });
139.         }

142.         {
143.             if (mediaPlayer == null || adaptiveContentModels == null || loggerControl == null)
144.                 return;
145. 
146.             mediaPlayer.AutoPlay = (bool)(sender as CheckBox).IsChecked;
147.         }
148. 
149.         private void ForceEquirectangularSphericalVideoFrameFormat()
150.         {
151.             if (mediaPlayer.PlaybackSession.PlaybackState == MediaPlaybackState.None ||
152.                 mediaPlayer.PlaybackSession.PlaybackState == MediaPlaybackState.Opening)
153.                 return;
154. 
155.             var mpsvp = mediaPlayer.PlaybackSession.SphericalVideoProjection;
156.             if (mpsvp == null)
157.                 return;
158. 
159.             if (mpsvp.FrameFormat != Windows.Media.MediaProperties.SphericalVideoFrameFormat.Equirectangular)
160.                 mpsvp.FrameFormat = Windows.Media.MediaProperties.SphericalVideoFrameFormat.Equirectangular;
161. 
162.             if (mpsvp.IsEnabled != true)
163.                 mpsvp.IsEnabled = true;
164. 
165.             mpsvp.HorizontalFieldOfViewInDegrees = 120;
166.         }
167. 
168.         private void SetSource_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
169.         {
170.             if (mediaPlayer == null || adaptiveContentModels == null || loggerControl == null)
171.                 return;
172.             
173.             // It is at this point that the MediaSource (within a MediaPlaybackItem) will be fully resolved.
174.             // For an AdaptiveMediaSource, this is the point at which media is first requested and parsed.
175.             mediaPlayer.Source = this.MediaPlaybackItem;
176.         }
177.     }

29.     /// See the README.md for discussion of this scenario.
30.     public sealed partial class Scenario2_EventHandlers : Page
31.     {
32.         private BitrateHelper bitrateHelper;
33. 
34.         public Scenario2_EventHandlers()
35.         {
36.             this.InitializeComponent();
37.         }
38. 
39.         protected override void OnNavigatedFrom(NavigationEventArgs e)
40.         {
41.             // Release handles on various media objects to ensure a quick clean-up
42.             ContentSelectorControl.MediaPlaybackItem = null;
43.             var mediaPlayer = mediaPlayerElement.MediaPlayer;
44.             if (mediaPlayer != null)
45.             {
46.                 mediaPlayerLogger?.Dispose();
47.                 mediaPlayerLogger = null;
48. 
49.                 UnregisterHandlers(mediaPlayer);
50. 
51.                 mediaPlayerElement.SetMediaPlayer(null);
52.                 mediaPlayer.Dispose();
53.             }
54.         }
55. 
56.         private void UnregisterHandlers(MediaPlayer mediaPlayer)
57.         {
58.             AdaptiveMediaSource adaptiveMediaSource = null;
59.             MediaPlaybackItem mpItem = mediaPlayer.Source as MediaPlaybackItem;
60.             if (mpItem != null)
61.             {
62.                 adaptiveMediaSource = mpItem.Source.AdaptiveMediaSource;
63.             }
64.             MediaSource source = mediaPlayer.Source as MediaSource;
65.             if (source != null)
66.             {
67.                 adaptiveMediaSource = source.AdaptiveMediaSource;
68.             }
69. 
70.             mediaPlaybackItemLogger?.Dispose();
71.             mediaPlaybackItemLogger = null;
72. 
73.             mediaSourceLogger?.Dispose();
74.             mediaSourceLogger = null;
75. 
76.             adaptiveMediaSourceLogger?.Dispose();
77.             adaptiveMediaSourceLogger = null;
78. 
79.             UnregisterForAdaptiveMediaSourceEvents(adaptiveMediaSource);
80.         }
81. 
82.         private void Page_OnLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
83.         {
84.             // Explicitly create the instance of MediaPlayer if you need to register for its events
85.             // (like MediaOpened / MediaFailed) prior to setting an IMediaPlaybackSource.
86.             var mediaPlayer = new MediaPlayer();
87. 
88.             // We use a helper class that logs all the events for the MediaPlayer:
89.             mediaPlayerLogger = new MediaPlayerLogger(LoggerControl, mediaPlayer);
90. 
91.             // Ensure we have PlayReady support, in case the user enters a DASH/PR Uri in the text box.
92.             var prHelper = new PlayReadyHelper(LoggerControl);
93.             prHelper.SetUpProtectionManager(mediaPlayer);
94.             mediaPlayerElement.SetMediaPlayer(mediaPlayer);
95. 
96.             // This Scenario focuses on event handling, mostly via the "Logger" helper classes
97.             // in the Shared folder.
98.             //
99.             // In addition, the App can also insert an IHttpFilter into the Windows.Web.Http stack
100.             // which is used by the AdaptiveMediaSource.  To do so, it must derive a class from
101.             // IHttpFilter, provide the HttpBaseProtocolFilter to that class to execute requests,
102.             // then use the derived filter as the constructor for the HttpClient.
103.             //
104.             // The HttpClient is then used in the constructor of the AdaptiveMediaSource.
105.             // The App should explicitly set:
106.             //   CacheControl.WriteBehavior = HttpCacheWriteBehavior.NoCache
107.             // When passing an HttpClient into the constructor of AdpativeMediaSource,
108.             // set this on the HttpBaseProtocolFilter.
109.             //
110.             // More than one IHttpFilter can be nested, for example, an App might
111.             // use a filter to modify web requests: see Scenario3 for details.
112.             //
113.             // In this scenario, we add an AdaptiveMediaSourceHttpFilterLogger to provide additional
114.             // verbose logging details for each request.
115.             var baseProtocolFilter = new HttpBaseProtocolFilter();
116.             baseProtocolFilter.CacheControl.WriteBehavior = HttpCacheWriteBehavior.NoCache;  // Always set WriteBehavior = NoCache
117.             var fistLevelFilter = new AdaptiveMediaSourceHttpFilterLogger(LoggerControl, baseProtocolFilter);
118.             var httpClient = new HttpClient(fistLevelFilter);
119. 
120. 
121.             ContentSelectorControl.Initialize(
122.                 mediaPlayer,
123.                 MainPage.ContentManagementSystemStub.Where(m => !m.Aes),
124.                 httpClient,
125.                 LoggerControl,
126.                 LoadSourceFromUriAsync);
127.         }
128. 
129. 
130.         #region Content Loading
131. 
132.         private async Task<MediaPlaybackItem> LoadSourceFromUriAsync(Uri uri, HttpClient httpClient = null)
133.         {
134.             UnregisterHandlers(mediaPlayerElement.MediaPlayer);
135. 
136.             AdaptiveMediaSourceCreationResult result = null;
137.             if (httpClient != null)
138.             {
139.                 result = await AdaptiveMediaSource.CreateFromUriAsync(uri, httpClient);
140.             }
141.             else
142.             {
143.                 result = await AdaptiveMediaSource.CreateFromUriAsync(uri);
144.             }
145. 
146.             MediaSource source;
147.             if (result.Status == AdaptiveMediaSourceCreationStatus.Success)
148.             {
149.                 var adaptiveMediaSource = result.MediaSource;
150. 
151.                 // We use a helper class that logs all the events for the AdaptiveMediaSource:
152.                 adaptiveMediaSourceLogger = new AdaptiveMediaSourceLogger(LoggerControl, adaptiveMediaSource);
153. 
154.                 // In addition to logging, we use the callbacks to update some UI elements in this scenario:
155.                 RegisterForAdaptiveMediaSourceEvents(adaptiveMediaSource);
156. 
157.                 // At this point, we have read the manifest of the media source, and all bitrates are known.
158.                 bitrateHelper = new BitrateHelper(adaptiveMediaSource.AvailableBitrates);
159. 
160.                 // The AdaptiveMediaSource chooses initial playback and download bitrates.
161.                 // See the Tuning scenario for examples of customizing these bitrates.
162.                 await UpdatePlaybackBitrateAsync(adaptiveMediaSource.CurrentPlaybackBitrate);
163.                 await UpdateDownloadBitrateAsync(adaptiveMediaSource.CurrentDownloadBitrate);
164. 
165.                 source = MediaSource.CreateFromAdaptiveMediaSource(adaptiveMediaSource);
166. 
167.             }
168.             else
169.             {
170.                 Log($"Error creating the AdaptiveMediaSource. Status: {result.Status}, ExtendedError.Message: {result.ExtendedError.Message}, ExtendedError.HResult: {result.ExtendedError.HResult.ToString("X8")}");
171. 
172.                 // In this scenario, we make a second attempt to load any URI that is
173.                 // not adaptive streaming as a progressive download URI:
174.                 Log($"Attempting to create a MediaSource from uri: {uri}");
175.                 source = MediaSource.CreateFromUri(uri);
176.             }
177. 
178.             // We use a helper class that logs all the events for the MediaSource:
179.             mediaSourceLogger = new MediaSourceLogger(LoggerControl, source);
180. 
181.             // You can save additional information in the CustomPropertySet for future retrieval.
182.             // Note: MediaSource.CustomProperties is a ValueSet and therefore can store
183.             // only serializable types.
184. 
185.             // Save the original Uri.
186.             source.CustomProperties["uri"] = uri.ToString();
187. 
188.             // You're likely to put a content tracking id into the CustomProperties.
189.             source.CustomProperties["contentId"] = Guid.NewGuid().ToString();
190. 
191.             var mpItem = new MediaPlaybackItem(source);
192. 
193.             // We use a helper class that logs all the events for the MediaPlaybackItem:
194.             mediaPlaybackItemLogger = new MediaPlaybackItemLogger(LoggerControl, mpItem);
195. 
196.             HideDescriptionOnSmallScreen();
197. 
198.             return mpItem;
199.         }
200. 
201.         private async void HideDescriptionOnSmallScreen()
202.         {
203.             // On small screens, hide the description text to make room for the video.
204.             // TODO[migrate-004]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
205.             await this.Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
206.             {
207.                 DescriptionText.Visibility = (ActualHeight < 500) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
208.             });
209.         }
210.         #endregion

213.         #region AdaptiveMediaSource Event Handlers

215.         private void RegisterForAdaptiveMediaSourceEvents(AdaptiveMediaSource adaptiveMediaSource)
216.         {
217.             adaptiveMediaSource.DownloadBitrateChanged += DownloadBitrateChanged;
218.             adaptiveMediaSource.PlaybackBitrateChanged += PlaybackBitrateChanged;
219.         }
220. 
221.         private void UnregisterForAdaptiveMediaSourceEvents(AdaptiveMediaSource adaptiveMediaSource)
222.         {
223.             if (adaptiveMediaSource != null)
224.             {
225.                 adaptiveMediaSource.DownloadBitrateChanged -= DownloadBitrateChanged;
226.                 adaptiveMediaSource.PlaybackBitrateChanged -= PlaybackBitrateChanged;
227.             }
228.         }
229.         private async void DownloadBitrateChanged(AdaptiveMediaSource sender, AdaptiveMediaSourceDownloadBitrateChangedEventArgs args)
230.         {
231.             uint downloadBitrate = args.NewValue;
232.             await UpdateDownloadBitrateAsync(downloadBitrate);
233.         }
234. 
235.         private async Task UpdateDownloadBitrateAsync(uint downloadBitrate)
236.         {
237.             // TODO[migrate-005]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
238.             await this.Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, new DispatchedHandler(() =>
239.             {
240.                 iconDownloadBitrate.Symbol = bitrateHelper.GetBitrateSymbol(downloadBitrate);
241.                 txtDownloadBitrate.Text = downloadBitrate.ToString();
242.             }));
243.         }
244. 
245.         private async void PlaybackBitrateChanged(AdaptiveMediaSource sender, AdaptiveMediaSourcePlaybackBitrateChangedEventArgs args)
246.         {
247.             uint playbackBitrate = args.NewValue;
248.             await UpdatePlaybackBitrateAsync(playbackBitrate);
249.         }
250. 
251.         private async Task UpdatePlaybackBitrateAsync(uint playbackBitrate)
252.         {
253.             // TODO[migrate-006]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
254.             await this.Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, new DispatchedHandler(() =>
255.             {
256.                 iconPlaybackBitrate.Symbol = bitrateHelper.GetBitrateSymbol(playbackBitrate);
257.                 txtPlaybackBitrate.Text = playbackBitrate.ToString();
258.             }));
259.         }
